CTI for CtiIrqPcAgrees   (9 frames)
==============================================================================

A CTI is a state that satisfies every assumed fact but still breaks the
target.  It is NOT a bug -- it is a state the real core can never reach.
The lemma to write is whatever fact rules this state out.
